\@@amsalign
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@[\pmsf(ii).]{The case $m+m^{\prime}>\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}$}.
Here, there are three subcases to consider.
\begin{ItemList}\itemize@item@{The subcase: $m\geq\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}$}.
Then, $m^{\prime}\geq\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}$ as well and
$\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}}\geq\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}}>r$. Thus setting
$m=\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}+s$ and $m^{\prime}=\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}+s^{\prime}$ with $s,s^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}$, we compute:
\@@amsalign
Since $e,\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}\geq 2$, the last expression above is clearly positive if $\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}\geq 3$. And if $\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}=2$, then by definition we must have
$c=c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}1}}}\leq r<c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}2}}}=c(1+e)$ and
$\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m+m^{\prime}}}}-N=c(1-e)+r(e-1)=(r-c)(e-1)\geq 0$.
Whence the conclusion that $N\leq\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m+m^{\prime}}}}$ as desired.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@{The subcase: $1\leq m<\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}\leq m^{\prime}$}.
Here again, it is already granted that $\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}}\geq\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}}>r$, while this time, $\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}=c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}<c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}}$ and
\@@amsalign
where the last inequality is clear because for $m=1$ we have $\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}1}}}=1$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@{The subcase: \mbox{$1\leq m\leq m^{\prime}<\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}$}}.
Here,
\begin{equation*}N=c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}+c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}}+\min(r,\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}})(e-1)\leq c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}+c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}}+r(e-1).\end{equation*}\mbox{Let $m+m^{\prime}=\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}+s$ for some $s\geq 1$; we have:}
\mbox{$\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m+m^{\prime}}}}=c(s+\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}})+sr(e-1)$} and
\mbox{$\overline{\mathrm{S}}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m+m^{\prime}}}}-N\geq c(s+\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}}-\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}}-\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}})+(s-1)r(e-1)$}.
Since $\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}}}}=1+\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}-1}}}e$ while $c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m}}},c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}m^{\prime}}}}\leq c\mathrm{S}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}\boldsymbol{m}_{{\cramped[\scriptstyle]{\mspace{-0.72mu}}}}-1}}}\leq r$, it follows that:
\@@amsalign
\end{ItemList}\end{ItemList}Thiscompletestheproofthattheproduct(in
